It checks `filterWords` against the title and `filterDomains` against the url and removes the story.\n- **`loadAlgolia()`** — used for the *"Top 24h" / "Top 48h" / "Top week"* views (`loadTopStoriesSince()` → Algolia) and for search. This path built the story list straight from the Algolia response and only applied the `hideClicked` rule — **no word/domain filtering at all**. Hence a story titled "…brain…" still showed up in the top 24/48 views even with `brain` as a filter.\n\nSecondarily, `filterWords`/`filterDomains` were only read in `onResume()` and a change to them never triggered a reload, so a newly added filter had no effect on the already-loaded list.\n\n## Patch (`app/src/main/java/com/simon/harmonichackernews/StoriesFragment.java`)\n\n1. Added a shared, null-safe helper `shouldFilterStory(Story)` that checks the title against filtered words and the url against filtered domains (skipping empty phrases so a trailing comma in the preference can\'t match everything).\n2. `loadAlgolia()` now filters the loaded stories: the Algolia results are filtered in a local list (so the header `Story` at index 0 is never touched) before being added to `stories`, and the parameter — previously unused — is now meaningfully named `applyFilters` (`true` for the top 24/48/week views, `false` for explicit user searches, where hiding the results the user asked for would be surprising).\n3. `loadStory()` now delegates to the same helper, which also removes a latent `NullPointerException` on `story.url`/`story.title` for stories without a url.\n4. Filters are initialized in `onCreateView()` (before the first refresh is kicked off) and, if the filter preferences changed while the user was in Settings, `onResume()` triggers `attemptRefresh()` so the new filters take effect immediately.\n\n`./gradlew clean assembleDebug` → **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 856, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 28027, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 856, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYPqMhagjkXZDYy2QDki', stop_reason=None, session_id='b1fbc73f-ca4e-4370-8c9a-a6c57cc6f122', uuid='f3e22e68-6cb0-4213-bfe2-abb017aed70c')
